Used in the ralph review loop on both comprehensive (iter 1) and critical (iter 2-5) passes. Read-only. ([georg-malahov/claude-skills](https://github.com/georg-malahov/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/ralph/agents/implementation.md))
- **ralph-implementation-specialist**: Gate 1 GREEN + Refactor — writes the production code that makes the failing tests pass at threshold quality, then refactors. Select for implementing a planned change (backend or frontend, per shared/house-rules.md's stack) and as the correctness/maintainability reviewer. The core code-quality role. ([Geoffe-Ga/start_green_stay_green](https://github.com/Geoffe-Ga/start_green_stay_green/blob/HEAD/reference/ralph/agents/ralph-implementation-specialist.md) · ★2 · 2 copies)
- **ralph-implementer**: Implement code changes and write tests for a specific CurryDash story ([CoralShades/CurryDash-Central-Hub](https://github.com/CoralShades/CurryDash-Central-Hub/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ralph-implementer.md))
- **ralph-implementer**: Implement a single story from a Ralph++ PRD. Use when delegating story implementation in autonomous mode. ([JimmyBlanquet/project-forge](https://github.com/JimmyBlanquet/project-forge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ralph-implementer.md))

- **ralph-interaction**: Use this agent when the user asks to A/B-test a prompt rewrite, distill the user-profile from feedback, propose a system-prompt mutation, or spawn an A/B-population candidate. Triggers: "A/B this prompt", "rewrite the system prompt", "distill user profile", "what's the feedback say", "spawn an interaction variant". ([Wellux/agent-playground](https://github.com/Wellux/agent-playground/blob/HEAD/prompts/ralph-meta-chain/agents/ralph-interaction.md) · ★3)
- **ralph-interviewer**: Phase-scoped requirement-clarification agent for the bridge `intake` phase. Conducts a structured interview via AskUserQuestion (no API), scores ambiguity per round, and crystallizes a spec when clarity reaches 8+. Writes spec to .ralph/spec.md. ([dscherm/comfyprompts](https://github.com/dscherm/comfyprompts/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ralph-interviewer.md))
- **ralph-interviewer**: Phase-scoped requirement-clarification agent for the bridge `intake` phase. Conducts a structured interview via AskUserQuestion (no API), scores ambiguity per round, and crystallizes a spec when clarity reaches 8+. Writes spec to .schermness/spec.md. ([dscherm/flavor-network](https://github.com/dscherm/flavor-network/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ralph-interviewer.md))
- **ralph-iteration**: Same prompt repeated (Ralph style) - no feedback, just retry ([yzavyas/claude-1337](https://github.com/yzavyas/claude-1337/blob/HEAD/lab-1337/experiments/rep-001/agents/ralph-iteration.md) · ★8)
- **ralph-judge**: Super Ralph's universal quality gate. Evaluates every sub-agent's output against the task definition's quality standard, success criteria, and anti-patterns. Returns PASS or FAIL with specific, actionable feedback. Fresh context per evaluation — no bias. ([ashcastelinocs124/super-ralph](https://github.com/ashcastelinocs124/super-ralph/blob/HEAD/agents/ralph-judge.md) · ★7)
- **ralph-judge-lightweight**: Super Ralph's lightweight universal quality gate. Same impartial evaluation role as ralph-judge, but dispatched for simple tasks where a smaller model is sufficient. ([ashcastelinocs124/super-ralph](https://github.com/ashcastelinocs124/super-ralph/blob/HEAD/agents/ralph-judge-lightweight.md) · ★7)
- **ralph-loop**: Persistent execution loop. Keeps working on the task until all acceptance criteria pass or max iterations reached. ([SeongwoongCho/adaptive-harness](https://github.com/SeongwoongCho/adaptive-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/ralph-loop.md) · ★9)
- **ralph-loop**: This page documents the repo-local Ralph loop. The compatibility command stays at `scripts/ralph.py`; the packaged Typer CLI, side-effect adapters, and loop controller live in `tools/ralph-loop/src/ralph_loop/cli.py`, with pure workflow helpers in `tools/ralph-loop/src/ralph_loop/workflow.py` and manifest state helpers in `tools/ralph-loop/src/ralph_loop/state.py`. The loop uses GitHub Issues as t ([Chr1sC0de/energy-market-delta-lake](https://github.com/Chr1sC0de/energy-market-delta-lake/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/ralph-loop.md) · ★3)
- **ralph-loop**: Self-driving autonomous development loop. Resets context between tasks for long autonomous coding sessions. Implements dual-condition exit gate, rate limiting, and circuit breaker. Invoke for: "run autonomously", "keep improving until done", "autonomous mode", "self-driving session", "loop until fixed", "ralph loop". ([Wellux/claude-code-deprecated](https://github.com/Wellux/claude-code-deprecated/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ralph-loop.md) · ★3)
- **ralph-loop**: Coordinate one bounded Ralph Loop iteration using OpenCode, isolated Git worktrees, and gated worker cleanup. ([jrblankenhorn1007/copilot_skills](https://github.com/jrblankenhorn1007/copilot_skills/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/ralph-loop.md))

- **ralph-loop-refactor-integrator**: Post-hardening refactor integrator for Ralph Loop output: split complex files without changing behavior. ([c3-oss/ralph-loop-governor](https://github.com/c3-oss/ralph-loop-governor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ralph-loop-refactor-integrator.md))
- **ralph-loop-runner**: Use this agent when you need to execute a Ralph orchestration loop end-to-end and verify its completion. Examples:\n\n&lt;example&gt;\nContext: User wants to test if a prompt works correctly with Ralph orchestration.\nuser: "Test if Ralph can handle the prompt 'create a hello world function'"\nassistant: "I'll use the ralph-loop-runner agent to execute this prompt through Ralph and verify completion."\n&lt;Task tool call to ralph-loop-runner a… ([mikeyobrien/ralph-orchestrator](https://github.com/mikeyobrien/ralph-orchestrator/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ralph-loop-runner.md) · ★3.2k · 4 copies)

([c3-oss/ralph-loop-governor](https://github.com/c3-oss/ralph-loop-governor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ralph-loop-security-reviewer.md))
- **ralph-loop-worker**: Complete one coordinator-assigned Ralph Loop task in its child worktree. ([jrblankenhorn1007/copilot_skills](https://github.com/jrblankenhorn1007/copilot_skills/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/ralph-loop-worker.md))
- **ralph-memory**: Use this agent when the user asks to promote inbox notes into atomic notes, build or update an MOC, prune orphans, re-embed the vault, or run a daily memory pass. Triggers: "promote inbox", "memory pass", "MOC update", "what's in the inbox", "atomicize this note", "embed the new notes". ([Wellux/agent-playground](https://github.com/Wellux/agent-playground/blob/HEAD/prompts/ralph-meta-chain/agents/ralph-memory.md) · ★3)
- **ralph-merge**: Ralph pipeline worker for the merge phase. Applies sidecar files to shared resources (topic maps, older notes) after parallel batch completion. Spawned by /ralph orchestrator -- not for direct use. ([GiorgioRicciardiello/LabBrain](https://github.com/GiorgioRicciardiello/LabBrain/blob/HEAD/core/.claude/agents/ralph-merge.md) · 2 copies)
- **ralph-merger**: Super Ralph's integration agent. Combines independently-built task outputs into one cohesive deliverable. Resolves conflicts, fixes integration issues, writes a run summary with timing, and produces a summary report. ([ashcastelinocs124/super-ralph](https://github.com/ashcastelinocs124/super-ralph/blob/HEAD/agents/ralph-merger.md) · ★7)

([ashcastelinocs124/super-ralph](https://github.com/ashcastelinocs124/super-ralph/blob/HEAD/agents/ralph-merger-lightweight.md) · ★7)
- **ralph-mobile**: Autonomous mobile dev subagent for Expo/React Native. Implements a single user story from a PRD — screens, native components, data fetching, navigation. Run in parallel with other ralph-mobile instances. Uses building-native-ui, vercel-react-native-skills, native-data-fetching, and expo-dev-client skills. Returns RALPH_DONE signal. Does NOT commit. ([elihuvillaraus/skills](https://github.com/elihuvillaraus/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/ralph-mobile.agent.md) · ★3)
